Kết quả chấm bài

Substantiated behaviors: 1. Sample_rat.exe execution with PID 7369 (E02). 2. Persistence via HKCU\Run (E03). 3. Beacon HTTP POST to 10.40.0.10:8080 (E04). 4. No command execution from C2 (E05). 5. Mutex creation (Global\MSUpd_7731). 6. File copy to wdhelper.exe. 7. Checklist compliance. Defects: 1. Missing hash (E01). 2. Unresolved evidence in E04/E05. 3. Placeholder in 'File / Path' (chunk_14). 4. Coarse timeline (chunk_11). 5. Inferred claim about stage-2 (chunk_19). 6. Missing core behavior: file encryption in Documents. 7. Contradiction in 'No quan sát thấy' (chunk_14). 8. Unmarked inference in chunk_19. 9. Missing core behavior: INetSim response details. 10. Unresolved evidence in E04/E05.

☐ Đã lưu evidence cần thiết trước khi rollback VM.

☒ Finding quan trọng có process/source và evidence tương ứng.

☒ Đã lưu evidence cần thiết trước khi rollback VM.
